Did this start recently, or has it been going on for a while?\n\nPatient: I'd say it's been going on for a while. This whole depressed stretch has been with me for about nine months now. At first, I could kind of manage it, but... it just became a lot harder to hide once my classes and work at the apprenticeship started slipping.\n\nDoctor: During these nine months of feeling depressed, have there been any periods where you felt noticeably better or more like yourself, even if just for a few days or a week?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's just been this same heavy feeling the whole time. I don't think I've had any breaks from it where I felt like my normal self, even for a couple of days. It's just been a constant thing for the last nine months.\n\nDoctor: During these nine months, have there been any days where you felt completely fine or back to normal, even if just for a single day?\n\nPatient: No, um... like I was saying, I really haven't had a single day like that. Not even for just one day. It's just been the exact same low feeling every day for the whole nine months.\n\nDoctor: During these nine months or even before that, have you had any periods where you felt unusually energetic, more talkative than usual, or like your mood was elevated or even euphoric?\n\nPatient: Actually, before this whole depressed stretch started, yeah... there were a few weeks where I felt almost too good. It was like Miami was just mine. But at the same time, um... I was getting really irritated, too.
